    @pytest.mark.migration
    async def test_p2_08_user_identities_migration_lifecycle() -> None:
        """Verify p2_08 upgrade applies user_identities with FORCE RLS, and downgrade reverts."""
        async with MigrationHarness() as harness:
            prev_rev = harness.previous_of("p2_08_user_identities")
            assert prev_rev == "p4_02b_oauth_clients_codes"
    
            # 1. Upgrade to p2_08_user_identities
            await harness.upgrade("p2_08_user_identities")
    
            # Verify tables exist with FORCE RLS
            for table_name in ("user_identities", "identity_link_intents"):
                table_info = await harness.fetch(
                    "SELECT c.relrowsecurity, c.relforcerowsecurity "
                    "FROM pg_class c "
                    "JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace "
                    "WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND c.relname = $1",
                    table_name,
                )
                assert len(table_info) == 1, f"Table {table_name} missing"
                assert table_info[0]["relrowsecurity"] is True
                assert table_info[0]["relforcerowsecurity"] is True
    
            # Verify kb_auth functions exist
            funcs = await harness.fetch(
                "SELECT proname FROM pg_proc p "
                "JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace "
                "WHERE n.nspname = 'kb_auth' AND proname LIKE 'identity_%'"
            )
            func_names = {r["proname"] for r in funcs}
            assert "identity_lookup" in func_names
            assert "identity_record_login" in func_names
            assert "identity_provision_new_user" in func_names
            assert "identity_list_for_principal" in func_names
            assert "identity_link_intent_create" in func_names
            assert "identity_link_consume_and_bind" in func_names
            assert "identity_unlink" in func_names
    
            # 2. Downgrade to previous revision
            await harness.downgrade(prev_rev)
    
            for table_name in ("user_identities", "identity_link_intents"):
                table_gone = await harness.fetch(
                    "SELECT c.relname FROM pg_class c "
                    "JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace "
                    "WHERE n.nspname = 'public' AND c.relname = $1",
                    table_name,
                )
                assert len(table_gone) == 0, f"Table {table_name} not dropped"
    
            funcs_gone = await harness.fetch(
                "SELECT proname FROM pg_proc p "
                "JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.pronamespace "
                "WHERE n.nspname = 'kb_auth' AND proname LIKE 'identity_%'"
            )
            assert len(funcs_gone) == 0
    
            # 3. Re-upgrade to p2_08_user_identities

    def _exec_single_context(
        self,
        dialect: Dialect,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        statement: Union[str, Compiled],
        parameters: Optional[_AnyMultiExecuteParams],
    ) -> CursorResult[Any]:
        """continue the _execute_context() method for a single DBAPI
        cursor.execute() or cursor.executemany() call.
    
        """
        if dialect.bind_typing is BindTyping.SETINPUTSIZES:
            generic_setinputsizes = context._prepare_set_input_sizes()
    
            if generic_setinputsizes:
                try:
                    dialect.do_set_input_sizes(
                        context.cursor, generic_setinputsizes, context
                    )
                except BaseException as e:
                    self._handle_dbapi_exception(
                        e, str(statement), parameters, None, context
                    )
    
        cursor, str_statement, parameters = (
            context.cursor,
            context.statement,
            context.parameters,
        )
    
        effective_parameters: Optional[_AnyExecuteParams]
    
        if not context.executemany:
            effective_parameters = parameters[0]
        else:
            effective_parameters = parameters
    
        if self._has_events or self.engine._has_events:
            for fn in self.dispatch.before_cursor_execute:
                str_statement, effective_parameters = fn(
                    self,
                    cursor,
                    str_statement,
                    effective_parameters,
                    context,
                    context.executemany,
                )
    
        if self._echo:
            self._log_info(str_statement)
    
            stats = context._get_cache_stats()
    
            if not self.engine.hide_parameters:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] %r",
                    stats,
                    sql_util._repr_params(
                        effective_parameters,
                        batches=10,
                        ismulti=context.executemany,
                    ),
                )
            else:
                self._log_info(
                    "[%s] [SQL parameters hidden due to hide_parameters=True]",
                    stats,
                )
    
        evt_handled: bool = False
        try:
            if context.execute_style is ExecuteStyle.EXECUTEMANY:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreMultiExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_executemany:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_executemany(
                        cursor,
                        str_statement,
                        effective_parameters,
                        context,
                    )
            elif not effective_parameters and context.no_parameters:
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute_no_params:
                        if fn(cursor, str_statement, context):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
                    self.dialect.do_execute_no_params(
                        cursor, str_statement, context
                    )
            else:
                effective_parameters = cast(
                    "_CoreSingleExecuteParams", effective_parameters
                )
                if self.dialect._has_events:
                    for fn in self.dialect.dispatch.do_execute:
                        if fn(
                            cursor,
                            str_statement,
                            effective_parameters,
                            context,
                        ):
                            evt_handled = True
                            break
                if not evt_handled:
>                   self.dialect.do_execute(
                        cursor, str_statement, effective_parameters, context

prev = '0|b', nxt = None

    def midpoint_rank(prev: str | None, nxt: str | None) -> str:  # noqa: C901, PLR0912, PLR0915
        """Compute a lexicographic midpoint between prev and nxt without trailing '0's.
    
        Ranks represent fractional values in [0, 1) expressed in base 62.
        Trailing '0' characters are forbidden so that infinite subdivisions remain possible.
        """
        if prev is not None:
            if not prev:
                msg = "prev rank must not be empty string"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            if prev.endswith("0"):
                msg = f"prev rank {prev!r} ends with forbidden '0'"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            for c in prev:
                if c not in _CHAR_TO_VAL:
                    msg = f"Invalid character {c!r} in prev rank"
>                   raise ValueError(msg)
E                   ValueError: Invalid character '|' in prev rank

fut = <coroutine object Process.wait at 0xf07c8ae32b00>, timeout = 3.0

    async def wait_for(fut, timeout):
        """Wait for the single Future or coroutine to complete, with timeout.
    
        Coroutine will be wrapped in Task.
    
        Returns result of the Future or coroutine.  When a timeout occurs,
        it cancels the task and raises TimeoutError.  To avoid the task
        cancellation, wrap it in shield().
    
        If the wait is cancelled, the task is also cancelled.
    
        If the task suppresses the cancellation and returns a value instead,
        that value is returned.
    
        This function is a coroutine.
        """
        # The special case for timeout <= 0 is for the following case:
        #
        # async def test_waitfor():
        #     func_started = False
        #
        #     async def func():
        #         nonlocal func_started
        #         func_started = True
        #
        #     try:
        #         await asyncio.wait_for(func(), 0)
        #     except asyncio.TimeoutError:
        #         assert not func_started
        #     else:
        #         assert False
        #
        # asyncio.run(test_waitfor())
    
    
        if timeout is not None and timeout <= 0:
            fut = ensure_future(fut)
    
            if fut.done():
                return fut.result()
    
            await _cancel_and_wait(fut)
            try:
                return fut.result()
            except exceptions.CancelledError as exc:
                raise TimeoutError from exc
    
        async with timeouts.timeout(timeout):
>           return await fut

    async def run_async(
        self,
        argv: list[str],
        *,
        stdin_bytes: bytes | None = None,
        timeout_seconds: float = 3.0,
        env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[int, bytes, bytes]:
        """Execute external command asynchronously with process group management."""
        proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
            *argv,
            stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE if stdin_bytes is not None else None,
            stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            env=env,
            start_new_session=True,
        )
        cap = MAX_STDOUT_BYTES + 1
    
        async def _read_capped(stream: asyncio.StreamReader | None) -> bytes:
            """Read incrementally; stop and kill the group once cap+1 bytes arrive."""
            if stream is None:
                return b""
            buf = bytearray()
            while len(buf) < cap:
                chunk = await stream.read(min(8192, cap - len(buf)))
                if not chunk:
                    break
                buf.extend(chunk)
            if len(buf) >= cap:

    async def run_async(
        self,
        argv: list[str],
        *,
        stdin_bytes: bytes | None = None,
        timeout_seconds: float = 3.0,
        env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[int, bytes, bytes]:
        """Execute external command asynchronously with process group management."""
        proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
            *argv,
            stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE if stdin_bytes is not None else None,
            stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
            env=env,
            start_new_session=True,
        )
        cap = MAX_STDOUT_BYTES + 1
    
        async def _read_capped(stream: asyncio.StreamReader | None) -> bytes:
            """Read incrementally; stop and kill the group once cap+1 bytes arrive."""
            if stream is None:
                return b""
            buf = bytearray()
            while len(buf) < cap:
                chunk = await stream.read(min(8192, cap - len(buf)))
                if not chunk:
                    break
                buf.extend(chunk)
            if len(buf) >= cap:
                with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                    os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), signal.SIGKILL)
            return bytes(buf)
    
        async def _feed_stdin() -> None:
            if stdin_bytes is None or proc.stdin is None:
                return
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                proc.stdin.write(stdin_bytes)
                await proc.stdin.drain()
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                proc.stdin.close()
    
        try:
            _, stdout, stderr = await asyncio.wait_for(
                asyncio.gather(
                    _feed_stdin(),
                    _read_capped(proc.stdout),
                    _read_capped(proc.stderr),
                ),
                timeout=timeout_seconds,
            )
            await asyncio.wait_for(proc.wait(), timeout=timeout_seconds)
        except TimeoutError as err:
            with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
                os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), signal.SIGKILL)
            await proc.wait()
            msg = "Command timed out"
>           raise TimeoutError(msg) from err
